<a class="btn btn-secondary">See more posts</a>
<span class="loader"></span>
</div>
<div class="lc-feed-links">
<div style="height: 300px">Welcome to LoveCrafts, your home to shop, share and dream in crafts.  Makers, discover thousands of <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/crochet/crochet-patterns">crochet patterns</a> and <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/knitting/knitting-patterns">knitting patterns</a> to inspire! Stock up your yarn stash with <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/yarns/yarns-by-brand/cascade-yarn">Cascade Yarns</a>,
<a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/yarns/yarns-by-brand/lion-brand-yarn">Lion Brand</a>, <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/yarns/yarns-by-brand/rowan-yarn">Rowan</a>, 
<a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/yarns/yarns-by-brand/paintbox-yarns-yarn">Paintbox Yarns</a> and more. Explore wonderful fibres like oh-so-warm <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/yarns/yarns-by-fibre/alpaca-yarn">alpaca yarn</a> and <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/yarns/yarns-by-fibre/merino-wool-yarn">merino wool</a>, or find yarns by weight, from classic <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/yarns/yarns-by-weight/aran-yarn">aran and worsted weight</a> to <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/yarns/yarns-by-weight/super-chunky-yarn">super chunky yarn</a>. What are you waiting for? Grab your <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/knitting/knitting-supplies-link/knitting-needles">needles</a>, <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/crochet/crochet-supplies-link/crochet-hooks">hooks</a> and <a href="https://www.lovecrafts.com/en-gb/l/cross-stitch-and-embroidery/cross-stitch-and-embroidery-supplies/cross-stitch-and-embroidery-accessories/cross-stitch-and-embroidery-hoops-and-frames">hoops</a> and start making!

<script>
        (function () {
            var selector = document.getElementById('multistore-selector-country');
            var country = document.cookie.match(/country=([^;]*)/);
            if (selector) {
                if (country) {
                    selector.value = country[1].trim();
                }

                selector.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
                    document.cookie = 'country=' + e.target.value + ';domain=.www.lovecrafts.com;path=/;max-age=31536000';
                    var delimiter = document.location.href.indexOf('?') > 0 ? '&' : '?';
                    var isCountryInUrl = /country=[a-zA-Z]{2}/gm.exec(document.location.href);
                    document.location.href = isCountryInUrl ?
                      document.location.href.replace(isCountryInUrl[0], 'country=' + e.target.value) :
                      document.location.href + delimiter + 'country=' + e.target.value;
                });
            }
        }());
    </script>
